# Rendering conformance run

[Feature printer/render/difference gallery](../accuracy/comparisons/features/README.md)

Suite: `render-conformance-v1`. 598 cases; 8 adapters.

This reports execution and equal-image relationships, not printer accuracy unless hash-matched printer references were supplied. A rendered image can still be wrong. Font coverage is device-dependent. Invalid inputs are kept separate.

| Library | Valid/boundary cases | Nonblank | Blank | Errors | Crashes | Timeouts | Printer IoU |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| codyps/zpl (Rust) | 579 | 566 | 11 | 2 | 0 | 0 | N/A |
| labelize (Rust) | 579 | 549 | 8 | 22 | 0 | 0 | N/A |
| zpl-forge (Rust) | 579 | 511 | 11 | 57 | 0 | 0 | N/A |
| go-zpl (Go) | 579 | 569 | 10 | 0 | 0 | 0 | N/A |
| zpl-rs (Rust → Go) | 579 | 569 | 10 | 0 | 0 | 0 | N/A |
| BinaryKits.Zpl (.NET) | 579 | 559 | 12 | 0 | 8 | 0 | N/A |
| ZPLr (TypeScript) | 579 | 569 | 10 | 0 | 0 | 0 | N/A |
| Labelary (SaaS) | 579 | 558 | 21 | 0 | 0 | 0 | N/A |


## Equal-raster relationships

Equality requires at least two nonblank successful outputs. Both blanks/errors are inconclusive; equality alone is not printer fidelity.

| Library | Relationship | Outcome |
| --- | --- | --- |
| codyps-zpl | inline-16x8 | equal |
| labelize | inline-16x8 | inconclusive |
| forge | inline-16x8 | inconclusive |

| zplr | inline-16x8 | inconclusive |
| labelary | inline-16x8 | equal |

| go | rle-fill | equal |
